task\+\_\+function\+\_\+call -\/ call a function on the cpu on which a task runs {\ttfamily }\+: the task to evaluate @func\+: the function to be called @info\+: the function call argument

Calls the function @func when the task is currently running. This might be on the current C\+PU, which just calls the function directly. This will retry due to any failures in \mbox{\hyperlink{kernel_2smp_8c_a01d34c6f7f3677880f1b4709c1c0922c}{smp\+\_\+call\+\_\+function\+\_\+single()}}, such as if the task\+\_\+cpu() goes offline concurrently.

returns @func return value or -\/E\+S\+R\+CH or -\/E\+N\+X\+IO when the process isn\textquotesingle{}t running Here is the call graph for this function\+:
